\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Comprehensive unit tests for all data structures using Google\+Test. 

Tests all 7 midterm algorithms\+: Recipe, Linked List, XOR Linked List, Sparse Matrix, Stack, Queue, Heap, Graph (BFS/\+DFS), Hash Table All tests run automatically without user interaction
